cat <<'EOF' > packages/vitest/src/node/sequencers/shard-analytics.ts
import type { TestSpecification } from '../test-specification'
import type { DurationFallbackStrategy, ShardStrategy } from '../../runtime/config'
import { slash } from '@vitest/utils/helpers'
import { relative } from 'pathe'
import { hash } from '../hash'
import type { CompiledAffinityRule } from './shard-affinity'
import { getAffinityShardIndex } from './shard-affinity'

export interface ShardOptions {
  root: string
  hasHistory: boolean
  strategy: ShardStrategy
  fallbackStrategy: DurationFallbackStrategy
  affinityRules: CompiledAffinityRule[]
  isolateSlowThreshold: number
  durations: Map<string, number>
}

export function getFileRelativePath(root: string, moduleId: string): string {
  return slash(relative(root, moduleId))
}

export function getFileDuration(path: string, durations: Map<string, number>): number {
  return durations.get(path) ?? 0
}

export function shardHash(files: TestSpecification[], shardCount: number): TestSpecification[][] {
  const sorted = files
    .map((spec) => {
      const fullPath = resolve(slash(config.root), slash(spec.moduleId))
      // placeholder - replaced in caller
      void fullPath
      return { spec, hash: '' }
    })
  void sorted
  return shardEqualSplit(files, shardCount)
}

function calculateShardRange(filesCount: number, index: number, count: number): [number, number] {
  const baseShardSize = Math.floor(filesCount / count)
  const remainderTestFilesCount = filesCount % count
  if (remainderTestFilesCount >= index) {
    const shardSize = baseShardSize + 1
    const shardStart = shardSize * (index - 1)
    const shardEnd = shardSize * index
    return [shardStart, shardEnd]
  }
  const shardStart = remainderTestFilesCount * (baseShardSize + 1) + (index - remainderTestFilesCount - 1) * baseShardSize
  const shardEnd = shardStart + baseShardSize
  return [shardStart, shardEnd]
}

export function shardEqualSplit(files: TestSpecification[], shardCount: number): TestSpecification[][] {
  const sorted = [...files].sort((a, b) => (a.moduleId < b.moduleId ? -1 : a.moduleId > b.moduleId ? 1 : 0))
  const shards: TestSpecification[][] = Array.from({ length: shardCount }, () => [])
  sorted.forEach((file, index) => {
    shards[index % shardCount].push(file)
  })
  return shards
}

export function shardTime(
  files: TestSpecification[],
  root: string,
  durations: Map<string, number>,
  shardCount: number,
): TestSpecification[][] {
  const sorted = [...files].sort((a, b) => {
    const durationDiff = getFileDuration(getFileRelativePath(root, b.moduleId), durations)
      - getFileDuration(getFileRelativePath(root, a.moduleId), durations)
    return durationDiff || (a.moduleId < b.moduleId ? -1 : a.moduleId > b.moduleId ? 1 : 0)
  })

  const loads = new Array<number>(shardCount).fill(0)
  const shards: TestSpecification[][] = Array.from({ length: shardCount }, () => [])

  for (const file of sorted) {
    let minIndex = 0
    for (let i = 1; i < shardCount; i++) {
      if (loads[i] < loads[minIndex]) {
        minIndex = i
      }
    }
    shards[minIndex].push(file)
    loads[minIndex] += getFileDuration(getFileRelativePath(root, file.moduleId), durations)
  }

  return shards
}

export function shardRoundRobin(
  files: TestSpecification[],
  root: string,
  durations: Map<string, number>,
  shardCount: number,
): TestSpecification[][] {
  const sorted = [...files].sort((a, b) => {
    const durationDiff = getFileDuration(getFileRelativePath(root, b.moduleId), durations)
      - getFileDuration(getFileRelativePath(root, a.moduleId), durations)
    if (durationDiff !== 0) {
      return durationDiff
    }
    return a.moduleId < b.moduleId ? -1 : a.moduleId > b.moduleId ? 1 : 0
  })

  const shards: TestSpecification[][] = Array.from({ length: shardCount }, () => [])
  let pointer = 0
  let direction = 1

  for (const file of sorted) {
    shards[pointer].push(file)

    pointer += direction
    if (pointer < 0) {
      pointer = 0
      direction = 1
    }
    else if (pointer >= shardCount) {
      pointer = shardCount - 1
      direction = -1
    }
  }

  return shards
}

export function shardAffinity(
  files: TestSpecification[],
  root: string,
  durations: Map<string, number>,
  shardCount: number,
  rules: CompiledAffinityRule[],
): TestSpecification[][] {
  const shards: TestSpecification[][] = Array.from({ length: shardCount }, () => [])
  const loads = new Array<number>(shardCount).fill(0)
  let matchedAny = false

  for (const file of files) {
    const path = getFileRelativePath(root, file.moduleId)
    const affinityIndex = getAffinityShardIndex(path, rules, shardCount)
    if (affinityIndex !== undefined) {
      shards[affinityIndex].push(file)
      loads[affinityIndex] += getFileDuration(path, durations)
      matchedAny = true
    }
    else {
      let minIndex = 0
      for (let i = 1; i < shardCount; i++) {
        if (loads[i] < loads[minIndex]) {
          minIndex = i
        }
      }
      shards[minIndex].push(file)
      loads[minIndex] += getFileDuration(path, durations)
    }
  }

  if (!matchedAny) {
    return shardTime(files, root, durations, shardCount)
  }

  return shards
}

export function shardByStrategy(
  files: TestSpecification[],
  strategy: ShardStrategy,
  root: string,
  durations: Map<string, number>,
  shardCount: number,
  affinityRules: CompiledAffinityRule[],
): TestSpecification[][] {
  switch (strategy) {
    case 'time':
      return shardTime(files, root, durations, shardCount)
    case 'round-robin':
      return shardRoundRobin(files, root, durations, shardCount)
    case 'affinity':
      return shardAffinity(files, root, durations, shardCount, affinityRules)
    case 'equal-split':
      return shardEqualSplit(files, shardCount)
    case 'hash':
    default:
      return shardEqualSplit(files, shardCount)
  }
}

function isolateSlowFiles(
  assignment: TestSpecification[][],
  files: TestSpecification[],
  root: string,
  durations: Map<string, number>,
  shardCount: number,
  strategy: ShardStrategy,
  affinityRules: CompiledAffinityRule[],
): TestSpecification[][] {
  const slow = files
    .filter(file => getFileDuration(getFileRelativePath(root, file.moduleId), durations) > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => {
      const durationDiff = getFileDuration(getFileRelativePath(root, b.moduleId), durations)
        - getFileDuration(getFileRelativePath(root, a.moduleId), durations)
      return durationDiff || (a.moduleId < b.moduleId ? -1 : a.moduleId > b.moduleId ? 1 : 0)
    })

  if (slow.length === 0) {
    return assignment
  }

  const slowSet = new Set(slow)
  const remaining = files.filter(file => !slowSet.has(file))
  const newAssignment: TestSpecification[][] = Array.from({ length: shardCount }, () => [])

  if (slow.length < shardCount) {
    slow.forEach((file, index) => {
      newAssignment[index].push(file)
    })
    const remainingAssignment = shardByStrategy(remaining, strategy, root, durations, shardCount, affinityRules)
    remainingAssignment.forEach((group, index) => {
      newAssignment[index].push(...group)
    })
  }
  else {
    for (let i = 0; i < shardCount - 1; i++) {
      newAssignment[i].push(slow[i])
    }
    newAssignment[shardCount - 1].push(...slow.slice(shardCount - 1))
    newAssignment[shardCount - 1].push(...remaining)
  }

  return newAssignment
}

/**
 * Shards files into `shardCount` groups using the configured strategy and
 * returns the complete assignment. `isolateSlowThreshold` is applied on top of
 * the selected strategy.
 */
export function shardFiles(
  files: TestSpecification[],
  shardCount: number,
  options: ShardOptions,
): TestSpecification[][] {
  const strategy: ShardStrategy = options.hasHistory
    ? options.strategy
    : (options.fallbackStrategy === 'equal-split' ? 'equal-split' : 'hash')

  let assignment = shardByStrategy(files, strategy, options.root, options.durations, shardCount, options.affinityRules)

  if (options.isolateSlowThreshold > 0) {
    assignment = isolateSlowFiles(
      assignment,
      files,
      options.root,
      options.durations,
      shardCount,
      strategy,
      options.affinityRules,
    )
  }

  return assignment
}

export function computeShardLoads(
  assignment: TestSpecification[][],
  root: string,
  durations: Map<string, number>,
): number[] {
  return assignment.map(group => group.reduce(
    (total, file) => total + getFileDuration(getFileRelativePath(root, file.moduleId), durations),
    0,
  ))
}

export function shouldWarnRebalance(loads: number[], threshold: number): boolean {
  if (threshold <= 0 || loads.length === 0) {
    return false
  }
  const maxLoad = Math.max(...loads)
  const minLoad = Math.min(...loads)
  if (maxLoad <= 0) {
    return false
  }
  return (minLoad / maxLoad) < threshold
}

cat <<'EOF' > packages/vitest/src/node/sequencers/BaseSequencer.ts
import type { Vitest } from '../core'
import type { TestSpecification } from '../test-specification'
import type { TestSequencer } from './types'
import { slash } from '@vitest/utils/helpers'
import { relative, resolve } from 'pathe'
import { hash } from '../hash'
import type { DurationHistory } from './duration-history'
import { readDurationHistory } from './duration-history'
import { getSmoothedDurations } from './duration-smoothing'
import { compileAffinityRules } from './shard-affinity'
import {
  computeShardLoads,
  getFileRelativePath,
  shouldWarnRebalance,
  shardFiles,
} from './shard-analytics'

export class BaseSequencer implements TestSequencer {
  protected ctx: Vitest

  constructor(ctx: Vitest) {
    this.ctx = ctx
  }

  // async so it can be extended by other sequelizers
  public async shard(files: TestSpecification[]): Promise<TestSpecification[]> {
    const { config } = this.ctx
    const { index, count } = config.shard!
    const { sequence } = config

    const historyPath = resolve(config.root, sequence.durationHistoryPath)
    const history: DurationHistory | null = await readDurationHistory(historyPath)
    const durations = getSmoothedDurations(history, sequence.durationSmoothing, sequence.durationHistoryTTL)
    const affinityRules = compileAffinityRules(sequence.shardAffinityRules)

    const assignment = shardFiles(files, count, {
      root: config.root,
      hasHistory: history !== null,
      strategy: sequence.shardStrategy,
      fallbackStrategy: sequence.durationFallbackStrategy,
      affinityRules,
      isolateSlowThreshold: sequence.isolateSlowThreshold,
      durations,
    })

    if (sequence.rebalanceThreshold > 0 && history !== null) {
      const loads = computeShardLoads(assignment, config.root, durations)
      if (shouldWarnRebalance(loads, sequence.rebalanceThreshold)) {
        const minLoad = Math.min(...loads)
        const maxLoad = Math.max(...loads)
        const ratio = maxLoad > 0 ? minLoad / maxLoad : 1
        this.ctx.logger.warn(
          `Shard balance is poor: ratio=${ratio.toFixed(2)} threshold=${sequence.rebalanceThreshold.toFixed(2)}. `
          + `Consider using a duration-aware sharding strategy like "time".`,
        )
      }
    }

    return assignment[index - 1] ?? []
  }

  // async so it can be extended by other sequelizers
  public async sort(files: TestSpecification[]): Promise<TestSpecification[]> {
    const { config } = this.ctx

    if (config.sequence.durationBasedSorting) {
      const historyPath = resolve(config.root, config.sequence.durationHistoryPath)
      const history = await readDurationHistory(historyPath)
      const durations = getSmoothedDurations(history, config.sequence.durationSmoothing, config.sequence.durationHistoryTTL)

      return [...files].sort((a, b) => {
        const pathA = getFileRelativePath(config.root, a.moduleId)
        const pathB = getFileRelativePath(config.root, b.moduleId)
        const hasA = durations.has(pathA)
        const hasB = durations.has(pathB)

        // files absent from history are sorted last
        if (hasA !== hasB) {
          return hasA ? -1 : 1
        }

        const durationA = hasA ? durations.get(pathA)! : 0
        const durationB = hasB ? durations.get(pathB)! : 0
        return durationB - durationA
      })
    }

    const cache = this.ctx.cache
    return [...files].sort((a, b) => {
      // "sequence.groupOrder" is higher priority
      const groupOrderDiff = a.project.config.sequence.groupOrder - b.project.config.sequence.groupOrder
      if (groupOrderDiff !== 0) {
        return groupOrderDiff
      }

      // Projects run sequential
      if (a.project.name !== b.project.name) {
        return a.project.name < b.project.name ? -1 : 1
      }

      // Isolated run first
      if (a.project.config.isolate && !b.project.config.isolate) {
        return -1
      }
      if (!a.project.config.isolate && b.project.config.isolate) {
        return 1
      }

      const keyA = `${a.project.name}:${relative(this.ctx.config.root, a.moduleId)}`
      const keyB = `${b.project.name}:${relative(this.ctx.config.root, b.moduleId)}`

      const aState = cache.getFileTestResults(keyA)
      const bState = cache.getFileTestResults(keyB)

      if (!aState || !bState) {
        const statsA = cache.getFileStats(keyA)
        const statsB = cache.getFileStats(keyB)

        // run unknown first
        if (!statsA || !statsB) {
          return !statsA && statsB ? -1 : !statsB && statsA ? 1 : 0
        }

        // run larger files first
        return statsB.size - statsA.size
      }

      // run failed first
      if (aState.failed && !bState.failed) {
        return -1
      }
      if (!aState.failed && bState.failed) {
        return 1
      }

      // run longer first
      return bState.duration - aState.duration
    })
  }

  // Calculate distributed shard range [start, end] distributed equally
  private calculateShardRange(filesCount: number, index: number, count: number): [number, number] {
    const baseShardSize = Math.floor(filesCount / count)
    const remainderTestFilesCount = filesCount % count
    if (remainderTestFilesCount >= index) {
      const shardSize = baseShardSize + 1
      const shardStart = shardSize * (index - 1)
      const shardEnd = shardSize * index
      return [shardStart, shardEnd]
    }

    const shardStart = remainderTestFilesCount * (baseShardSize + 1) + (index - remainderTestFilesCount - 1) * baseShardSize
    const shardEnd = shardStart + baseShardSize
    return [shardStart, shardEnd]
  }
}
EOF

p = 'packages/vitest/src/node/config/resolveConfig.ts'

helper = '''
function resolveSequenceConfig(sequence: any): void {
  if (typeof sequence !== \'object\' || sequence === null) {
    throw new Error(\'Invalid "sequence" config: expected an object\')
  }

  const fail = (field: string, message: string): never => {
    throw new Error(`Invalid "sequence.${field}" config: ${message}`)
  }

  const shardStrategies = [\'hash\', \'time\', \'round-robin\', \'affinity\']
  const smoothingStrategies = [\'latest\', \'average\', \'p95\', \'median\']
  const fallbackStrategies = [\'hash\', \'equal-split\']

  if (sequence.shardStrategy !== undefined && !shardStrategies.includes(sequence.shardStrategy)) {
    fail(\'shardStrategy\', `expected one of "${shardStrategies.join(\'", "\')}", received "${sequence.shardStrategy}"`)
  }
  if (sequence.balanceShardsByTime !== undefined && typeof sequence.balanceShardsByTime !== \'boolean\') {
    fail(\'balanceShardsByTime\', \'expected a boolean\')
  }
  if (sequence.recordFileDurations !== undefined && typeof sequence.recordFileDurations !== \'boolean\') {
    fail(\'recordFileDurations\', \'expected a boolean\')
  }
  if (sequence.durationBasedSorting !== undefined && typeof sequence.durationBasedSorting !== \'boolean\') {
    fail(\'durationBasedSorting\', \'expected a boolean\')
  }
  if (sequence.durationHistoryTTL !== undefined && (typeof sequence.durationHistoryTTL !== \'number\' || !Number.isFinite(sequence.durationHistoryTTL) || sequence.durationHistoryTTL < 0)) {
    fail(\'durationHistoryTTL\', \'expected a finite number greater than or equal to 0\')
  }
  if (sequence.durationHistoryPath !== undefined) {
    if (typeof sequence.durationHistoryPath !== \'string\' || sequence.durationHistoryPath.trim() === \'\') {
      fail(\'durationHistoryPath\', \'expected a non-empty string\')
    }
    else if (sequence.durationHistoryPath !== sequence.durationHistoryPath.trim()) {
      fail(\'durationHistoryPath\', \'must not have leading or trailing whitespace\')
    }
  }
  if (sequence.durationHistoryMaxRuns !== undefined && (!Number.isInteger(sequence.durationHistoryMaxRuns) || sequence.durationHistoryMaxRuns < 1)) {
    fail(\'durationHistoryMaxRuns\', \'expected an integer greater than or equal to 1\')
  }
  if (sequence.durationSmoothing !== undefined && !smoothingStrategies.includes(sequence.durationSmoothing)) {
    fail(\'durationSmoothing\', `expected one of "${smoothingStrategies.join(\'", "\')}", received "${sequence.durationSmoothing}"`)
  }
  if (sequence.shardAffinityRules !== undefined) {
    if (!Array.isArray(sequence.shardAffinityRules)) {
      fail(\'shardAffinityRules\', \'expected an array\')
    }
    sequence.shardAffinityRules.forEach((rule: any, index: number) => {
      if (typeof rule !== \'object\' || rule === null) {
        fail(\'shardAffinityRules\', `rule at index ${index} must be an object`)
      }
      if (typeof rule.pattern !== \'string\') {
        fail(\'shardAffinityRules\', `rule at index ${index} must have a string "pattern"`)
      }
      if (!Number.isInteger(rule.shardIndex) || rule.shardIndex < 0) {
        fail(\'shardAffinityRules\', `rule at index ${index} must have an integer "shardIndex" greater than or equal to 0`)
      }
    })
  }
  if (sequence.rebalanceThreshold !== undefined && (typeof sequence.rebalanceThreshold !== \'number\' || !Number.isFinite(sequence.rebalanceThreshold) || sequence.rebalanceThreshold < 0 || sequence.rebalanceThreshold > 1)) {
    fail(\'rebalanceThreshold\', \'expected a number between 0 and 1 inclusive\')
  }
  if (sequence.isolateSlowThreshold !== undefined && (typeof sequence.isolateSlowThreshold !== \'number\' || !Number.isFinite(sequence.isolateSlowThreshold) || sequence.isolateSlowThreshold < 0)) {
    fail(\'isolateSlowThreshold\', \'expected a finite number greater than or equal to 0\')
  }
  if (sequence.durationFallbackStrategy !== undefined && !fallbackStrategies.includes(sequence.durationFallbackStrategy)) {
    fail(\'durationFallbackStrategy\', `expected one of "${fallbackStrategies.join(\'", "\')}", received "${sequence.durationFallbackStrategy}"`)
  }
}
'''

import type { TestSpecification } from '../test-specification'
import type { DurationFallbackStrategy, ShardStrategy } from '../../runtime/config'
import { slash } from '@vitest/utils/helpers'
import { relative, resolve } from 'pathe'
import { hash } from '../hash'
import type { CompiledAffinityRule } from './shard-affinity'
import { getAffinityShardIndex } from './shard-affinity'

export interface ShardOptions {
  root: string
  hasHistory: boolean
  strategy: ShardStrategy
  fallbackStrategy: DurationFallbackStrategy
  affinityRules: CompiledAffinityRule[]
  isolateSlowThreshold: number
  durations: Map<string, number>
}

export function getFileRelativePath(root: string, moduleId: string): string {
  return slash(relative(root, moduleId))
}

export function getFileDuration(path: string, durations: Map<string, number>): number {
  return durations.get(path) ?? 0
}

export function shardHash(
  files: TestSpecification[],
  root: string,
  shardCount: number,
): TestSpecification[][] {
  const sorted = [...files]
    .map((spec) => {
      const fullPath = resolve(slash(root), slash(spec.moduleId))
      const specPath = fullPath?.slice(root.length)
      return {
        spec,
        hash: hash('sha1', specPath, 'hex'),
      }
    })
    .sort((a, b) => (a.hash < b.hash ? -1 : a.hash > b.hash ? 1 : 0))
    .map(({ spec }) => spec)

  const shards: TestSpecification[][] = Array.from({ length: shardCount }, () => [])
  for (let index = 1; index <= shardCount; index++) {
    const [start, end] = calculateShardRange(sorted.length, index, shardCount)
    shards[index - 1] = sorted.slice(start, end)
  }
  return shards
}

function calculateShardRange(filesCount: number, index: number, count: number): [number, number] {
  const baseShardSize = Math.floor(filesCount / count)
  const remainderTestFilesCount = filesCount % count
  if (remainderTestFilesCount >= index) {
    const shardSize = baseShardSize + 1
    const shardStart = shardSize * (index - 1)
    const shardEnd = shardSize * index
    return [shardStart, shardEnd]
  }
  const shardStart = remainderTestFilesCount * (baseShardSize + 1) + (index - remainderTestFilesCount - 1) * baseShardSize
  const shardEnd = shardStart + baseShardSize
  return [shardStart, shardEnd]
}

export function shardEqualSplit(files: TestSpecification[], shardCount: number): TestSpecification[][] {
  const sorted = [...files].sort((a, b) => (a.moduleId < b.moduleId ? -1 : a.moduleId > b.moduleId ? 1 : 0))
  const shards: TestSpecification[][] = Array.from({ length: shardCount }, () => [])
  sorted.forEach((file, index) => {
    shards[index % shardCount].push(file)
  })
  return shards
}

export function shardTime(
  files: TestSpecification[],
  root: string,
  durations: Map<string, number>,
  shardCount: number,
): TestSpecification[][] {
  const sorted = [...files].sort((a, b) => {
    const durationDiff = getFileDuration(getFileRelativePath(root, b.moduleId), durations)
      - getFileDuration(getFileRelativePath(root, a.moduleId), durations)
    return durationDiff || (a.moduleId < b.moduleId ? -1 : a.moduleId > b.moduleId ? 1 : 0)
  })

  const loads = new Array<number>(shardCount).fill(0)
  const shards: TestSpecification[][] = Array.from({ length: shardCount }, () => [])

  for (const file of sorted) {
    let minIndex = 0
    for (let i = 1; i < shardCount; i++) {
      if (loads[i] < loads[minIndex]) {
        minIndex = i
      }
    }
    shards[minIndex].push(file)
    loads[minIndex] += getFileDuration(getFileRelativePath(root, file.moduleId), durations)
  }

  return shards
}

export function shardRoundRobin(
  files: TestSpecification[],
  root: string,
  durations: Map<string, number>,
  shardCount: number,
): TestSpecification[][] {
  const sorted = [...files].sort((a, b) => {
    const durationDiff = getFileDuration(getFileRelativePath(root, b.moduleId), durations)
      - getFileDuration(getFileRelativePath(root, a.moduleId), durations)
    if (durationDiff !== 0) {
      return durationDiff
    }
    return a.moduleId < b.moduleId ? -1 : a.moduleId > b.moduleId ? 1 : 0
  })

  const shards: TestSpecification[][] = Array.from({ length: shardCount }, () => [])
  let pointer = 0
  let direction = 1

  for (const file of sorted) {
    shards[pointer].push(file)

    pointer += direction
    if (pointer < 0) {
      pointer = 0
      direction = 1
    }
    else if (pointer >= shardCount) {
      pointer = shardCount - 1
      direction = -1
    }
  }

  return shards
}

export function shardAffinity(
  files: TestSpecification[],
  root: string,
  durations: Map<string, number>,
  shardCount: number,
  rules: CompiledAffinityRule[],
): TestSpecification[][] {
  const shards: TestSpecification[][] = Array.from({ length: shardCount }, () => [])
  const loads = new Array<number>(shardCount).fill(0)
  let matchedAny = false

  for (const file of files) {
    const path = getFileRelativePath(root, file.moduleId)
    const affinityIndex = getAffinityShardIndex(path, rules, shardCount)
    if (affinityIndex !== undefined) {
      shards[affinityIndex].push(file)
      loads[affinityIndex] += getFileDuration(path, durations)
      matchedAny = true
    }
    else {
      let minIndex = 0
      for (let i = 1; i < shardCount; i++) {
        if (loads[i] < loads[minIndex]) {
          minIndex = i
        }
      }
      shards[minIndex].push(file)
      loads[minIndex] += getFileDuration(path, durations)
    }
  }

  if (!matchedAny) {
    return shardTime(files, root, durations, shardCount)
  }

  return shards
}

export function shardByStrategy(
  files: TestSpecification[],
  strategy: ShardStrategy,
  root: string,
  durations: Map<string, number>,
  shardCount: number,
  affinityRules: CompiledAffinityRule[],
): TestSpecification[][] {
  switch (strategy) {
    case 'time':
      return shardTime(files, root, durations, shardCount)
    case 'round-robin':
      return shardRoundRobin(files, root, durations, shardCount)
    case 'affinity':
      return shardAffinity(files, root, durations, shardCount, affinityRules)
    case 'equal-split':
      return shardEqualSplit(files, shardCount)
    case 'hash':
      return shardHash(files, root, shardCount)
    default:
      return shardEqualSplit(files, shardCount)
  }
}

function isolateSlowFiles(
  assignment: TestSpecification[][],
  files: TestSpecification[],
  root: string,
  durations: Map<string, number>,
  threshold: number,
  shardCount: number,
  strategy: ShardStrategy,
  affinityRules: CompiledAffinityRule[],
): TestSpecification[][] {
  const slow = files
    .filter(file => getFileDuration(getFileRelativePath(root, file.moduleId), durations) > threshold)
    .sort((a, b) => {
      const durationDiff = getFileDuration(getFileRelativePath(root, b.moduleId), durations)
        - getFileDuration(getFileRelativePath(root, a.moduleId), durations)
      return durationDiff || (a.moduleId < b.moduleId ? -1 : a.moduleId > b.moduleId ? 1 : 0)
    })

  if (slow.length === 0) {
    return assignment
  }

  const slowSet = new Set(slow)
  const remaining = files.filter(file => !slowSet.has(file))
  const newAssignment: TestSpecification[][] = Array.from({ length: shardCount }, () => [])

  if (slow.length < shardCount) {
    slow.forEach((file, index) => {
      newAssignment[index].push(file)
    })
    const remainingAssignment = shardByStrategy(remaining, strategy, root, durations, shardCount, affinityRules)
    remainingAssignment.forEach((group, index) => {
      newAssignment[index].push(...group)
    })
  }
  else {
    for (let i = 0; i < shardCount - 1; i++) {
      newAssignment[i].push(slow[i])
    }
    newAssignment[shardCount - 1].push(...slow.slice(shardCount - 1))
    newAssignment[shardCount - 1].push(...remaining)
  }

  return newAssignment
}

/**
 * Shards files into `shardCount` groups using the configured strategy and
 * returns the complete assignment. `isolateSlowThreshold` is applied on top of
 * the selected strategy.
 */
export function shardFiles(
  files: TestSpecification[],
  shardCount: number,
  options: ShardOptions,
): TestSpecification[][] {
  const strategy: ShardStrategy = options.hasHistory
    ? options.strategy
    : (options.fallbackStrategy === 'equal-split' ? 'equal-split' : 'hash')

  let assignment = shardByStrategy(files, strategy, options.root, options.durations, shardCount, options.affinityRules)

  if (options.isolateSlowThreshold > 0) {
    assignment = isolateSlowFiles(
      assignment,
      files,
      options.root,
      options.durations,
      options.isolateSlowThreshold,
      shardCount,
      strategy,
      options.affinityRules,
    )
  }

  return assignment
}

export function computeShardLoads(
  assignment: TestSpecification[][],
  root: string,
  durations: Map<string, number>,
): number[] {
  return assignment.map(group => group.reduce(
    (total, file) => total + getFileDuration(getFileRelativePath(root, file.moduleId), durations),
    0,
  ))
}

export function shouldWarnRebalance(loads: number[], threshold: number): boolean {
  if (threshold <= 0 || loads.length === 0) {
    return false
  }
  const maxLoad = Math.max(...loads)
  const minLoad = Math.min(...loads)
  if (maxLoad <= 0) {
    return false
  }
  return (minLoad / maxLoad) < threshold
}

There are a couple of issues:
1. In `shardHash`, the `specPath` could be `undefined` (if fullPath is undefined) but `hash('sha1', specPath, 'hex')` expects `crypto.BinaryLike`
which can be string; undefined would be type error. In the original code `fullPath?.slice(...)` could also be undefined and passed to hash; TS may
complain. Let me check the original - it used `hash('sha1', specPath, 'hex')` where `specPath` type is `string | undefined`. Since crypto.hash accepts
`BinaryLike = string | ... | Buffer`, undefined isn't allowed. The original passed it directly too, so maybe TS allowed it? Let me check if there's a
type issue. Actually original:
```
const fullPath = resolve(slash(config.root), slash(spec.moduleId))
const specPath = fullPath?.slice(config.root.length)
return { spec, hash: hash('sha1', specPath, 'hex') }
```
If `fullPath` is string (resolve returns string), then `fullPath?.slice` returns string | undefined, and TS would complain. But maybe `resolve`
returns string and `fullPath?.slice` is still `string`? No, optional chaining on a non-nullable type returns the same type (no `undefined` added?
Actually optional chaining adds undefined to the type). Hmm. The original code compiled, so maybe TS is lenient with `crypto.hash` overload or
`specPath` inferred as string. Let me not worry and just match original.

match remaining, it falls back to time. Good.

import type { ApiConfig } from '../types/config'
import type { CliOptions } from './cli-api'
import { defaultBrowserPort, defaultPort } from '../../constants'
import { ReportersMap } from '../reporters'

type NestedOption<T, V = Extract<T, Record<string, any>>> = V extends
  | never
  | RegExp
  | unknown[]
  ? never
  : V

export type CLIOption<Value> = {
  description: string | null
  alias?: string
  shorthand?: string
  default?: unknown
  transform?: (value: unknown) => unknown
  array?: boolean
  normalize?: boolean
} & (NestedOption<Value> extends never // require subcommands for nested options
  ? object
  : { subcommands: CLIOptions<NestedOption<Value>> | null })
  // require argument for non-boolean options
& (NonNullable<Value> extends boolean ? object : { argument: string })

export type CLIOptions<Config extends object> = {
  [Key in keyof Config as NonNullable<Config[Key]> extends Function
    ? never
    : Key]-?: CLIOption<Config[Key]> | null;
}

function watermarkTransform(value: unknown) {
  if (typeof value === 'string') {

}

  if (typeof value === 'boolean') {

FUNCTION bash

import {
  shardFiles,
  shardRoundRobin,
  shardTime,
  shardEqualSplit,
  computeShardLoads,
} from './packages/vitest/src/node/sequencers/shard-analytics'
import { compileAffinityRules } from './packages/vitest/src/node/sequencers/shard-affinity'
import { getSmoothedDurations, smoothObservations } from './packages/vitest/src/node/sequencers/duration-smoothing'

function spec(path: string) {
  return { moduleId: `/project/${path}`, project: {} } as any
}

function names(groups: any[][]) {
  return groups.map(g => g.map((f: any) => f.moduleId.split('/').pop()))
}

const files = ['a.ts', 'b.ts', 'c.ts', 'd.ts', 'e.ts'].map(spec)
const durations = new Map([
  ['a.ts', 100],
  ['b.ts', 80],
  ['c.ts', 60],
  ['d.ts', 40],
  ['e.ts', 20],
])

// time LPT
const time = shardTime(files, '/project', durations, 2)
console.log('time:', names(time), 'loads:', computeShardLoads(time, '/project', durations))

// round robin
const rr = shardRoundRobin(files, '/project', durations, 3)
console.log('rr:', names(rr), 'loads:', computeShardLoads(rr, '/project', durations))

// equal split
const eq = shardEqualSplit(files, 2)
console.log('eq:', names(eq))

// smoothing
console.log('smooth avg:', smoothObservations([{duration:10, recordedAt:1},{duration:20, recordedAt:2}], 'average'))
console.log('smooth latest:', smoothObservations([{duration:10, recordedAt:1},{duration:20, recordedAt:2}], 'latest'))
console.log('smooth p95:', smoothObservations([{duration:10, recordedAt:1},{duration:20, recordedAt:2},{duration:30, recordedAt:3}], 'p95'))
console.log('smooth median even:', smoothObservations([{duration:10, recordedAt:1},{duration:30, recordedAt:2}], 'median'))
console.log('smooth median odd:', smoothObservations([{duration:10, recordedAt:1},{duration:20, recordedAt:2},{duration:30, recordedAt:3}], 'median'))

// affinity
const rules = compileAffinityRules([{pattern: 'a.ts', shardIndex: 1}, {pattern: 'b.ts', shardIndex: 0}])
console.log('affinity:', names(shardFiles(files, 2, {
  root: '/project', hasHistory: true, strategy: 'affinity', fallbackStrategy: 'hash', affinityRules: rules, isolateSlowThreshold: 0, durations,
})))

// no history fallback equal-split
console.log('fallback equal:', names(shardFiles(files, 2, {
  root: '/project', hasHistory: false, strategy: 'time', fallbackStrategy: 'equal-split', affinityRules: [], isolateSlowThreshold: 0, durations,
})))

// slow isolation: slow.length < shardCount
const slowFiles = [spec('slow1.ts'), spec('slow2.ts'), spec('fast1.ts'), spec('fast2.ts')]
const d2 = new Map([['slow1.ts', 1000], ['slow2.ts', 900], ['fast1.ts', 10], ['fast2.ts', 10]])
console.log('slow iso (<count):', names(shardFiles(slowFiles, 3, {
  root: '/project', hasHistory: true, strategy: 'time', fallbackStrategy: 'hash', affinityRules: [], isolateSlowThreshold: 100, durations: d2,
})))

// slow isolation: slow.length >= shardCount
const slowFiles2 = [spec('slow1.ts'), spec('slow2.ts'), spec('slow3.ts'), spec('slow4.ts'), spec('fast1.ts')]
const d3 = new Map([['slow1.ts', 1000], ['slow2.ts', 900], ['slow3.ts', 800], ['slow4.ts', 700], ['fast1.ts', 10]])
console.log('slow iso (>=count):', names(shardFiles(slowFiles2, 3, {
  root: '/project', hasHistory: true, strategy: 'time', fallbackStrategy: 'hash', affinityRules: [], isolateSlowThreshold: 100, durations: d3,
})))

// smoothing with TTL
const history = {
  'a.ts': { duration: 100, recordedAt: 1000 },
  'b.ts': { observations: [{duration: 10, recordedAt: 1}, {duration: 20, recordedAt: 200}] },
  'legacy.ts': 5000,
}
const smoothed = getSmoothedDurations(history as any, 'latest', 100, 300)
console.log('smoothed ttl:', [...smoothed.entries()])
EOF

     1	import type { DurationSmoothingStrategy } from '../../runtime/config'
     2	import type { DurationHistory, DurationObservation } from './duration-history'
     3	import { getHistoryObservations } from './duration-history'
     4	
     5	/**
     6	 * Smooths a list of duration observations into a single duration value.
     7	 */
     8	export function smoothObservations(
     9	  observations: DurationObservation[],
    10	  strategy: DurationSmoothingStrategy,
    11	): number {
    12	  if (observations.length === 0) {
    13	    return 0
    14	  }
    15	
    16	  switch (strategy) {
    17	    case 'latest': {
    18	      return observations.reduce((latest, observation) => (
    19	        observation.recordedAt > latest.recordedAt ? observation : latest
    20	      ), observations[0]).duration
    21	    }
    22	    case 'average': {
    23	      const sum = observations.reduce((total, observation) => total + observation.duration, 0)
    24	      return Math.round(sum / observations.length)
    25	    }
    26	    case 'p95': {
    27	      const sorted = observations
    28	        .map(observation => observation.duration)
    29	        .sort((a, b) => a - b)
    30	      const index = Math.ceil(0.95 * sorted.length) - 1
    31	      return sorted[index]
    32	    }
    33	    case 'median': {
    34	      const sorted = observations
    35	        .map(observation => observation.duration)
    36	        .sort((a, b) => a - b)
    37	      const middle = Math.floor(sorted.length / 2)
    38	      if (sorted.length % 2 === 0) {
    39	        return Math.floor((sorted[middle - 1] + sorted[middle]) / 2)
    40	      }
    41	      return sorted[middle]
    42	    }
    43	  }
    44	}
    45	
    46	/**
    47	 * Builds a map of relative file paths to smoothed durations. Expired
    48	 * observations (based on `ttl`) are excluded. Files missing from the history
    49	 * are not present in the returned map and should be treated as duration 0.
    50	 */
    51	export function getSmoothedDurations(
    52	  history: DurationHistory | null,
    53	  strategy: DurationSmoothingStrategy,
    54	  ttl: number,
    55	  now = Date.now(),
    56	): Map<string, number> {
    57	  const durations = new Map<string, number>()
    58	  if (!history) {
    59	    return durations
    60	  }
    61	
    62	  for (const path of Object.keys(history)) {
    63	    const observations = getHistoryObservations(history, path).filter((observation) => {
    64	      if (observation.recordedAt === 0) {
    65	        return true
    66	      }
    67	      return ttl <= 0 || observation.recordedAt >= now - ttl
    68	    })
    69	
    70	    if (observations.length > 0) {
    71	      durations.set(path, smoothObservations(observations, strategy))
    72	    }
    73	  }
    74	
    75	  return durations
    76	}

CALL s78_c0 TOOL_ID call_00_VnN0qj1tbLroemBMhOJ62208
FUNCTION bash

command:
cd /app && cat > test-shard.tmp.ts <<'EOF'
import {
  shardFiles,
  shardRoundRobin,
  shardTime,
  shardEqualSplit,
  computeShardLoads,
} from './packages/vitest/src/node/sequencers/shard-analytics'
import { compileAffinityRules } from './packages/vitest/src/node/sequencers/shard-affinity'
import { getSmoothedDurations, smoothObservations } from './packages/vitest/src/node/sequencers/duration-smoothing'

function spec(path: string) {
  return { moduleId: `/project/${path}`, project: {} } as any
}

function names(groups: any[][]) {
  return groups.map(g => g.map((f: any) => f.moduleId.split('/').pop()))
}

const files = ['a.ts', 'b.ts', 'c.ts', 'd.ts', 'e.ts'].map(spec)
const durations = new Map([
  ['a.ts', 100],
  ['b.ts', 80],
  ['c.ts', 60],
  ['d.ts', 40],
  ['e.ts', 20],
])

// time LPT
const time = shardTime(files, '/project', durations, 2)
console.log('time:', names(time), 'loads:', computeShardLoads(time, '/project', durations))

// round robin
const rr = shardRoundRobin(files, '/project', durations, 3)
console.log('rr:', names(rr), 'loads:', computeShardLoads(rr, '/project', durations))

// equal split
const eq = shardEqualSplit(files, 2)
console.log('eq:', names(eq))

// smoothing
console.log('smooth avg:', smoothObservations([{duration:10, recordedAt:1},{duration:20, recordedAt:2}], 'average'))
console.log('smooth latest:', smoothObservations([{duration:10, recordedAt:1},{duration:20, recordedAt:2}], 'latest'))
console.log('smooth p95:', smoothObservations([{duration:10, recordedAt:1},{duration:20, recordedAt:2},{duration:30, recordedAt:3}], 'p95'))
console.log('smooth median even:', smoothObservations([{duration:10, recordedAt:1},{duration:30, recordedAt:2}], 'median'))
console.log('smooth median odd:', smoothObservations([{duration:10, recordedAt:1},{duration:20, recordedAt:2},{duration:30, recordedAt:3}], 'median'))

// affinity
const rules = compileAffinityRules([{pattern: 'a.ts', shardIndex: 1}, {pattern: 'b.ts', shardIndex: 0}])
console.log('affinity:', names(shardFiles(files, 2, {
  root: '/project', hasHistory: true, strategy: 'affinity', fallbackStrategy: 'hash', affinityRules: rules, isolateSlowThreshold: 0, durations,
})))

// no history fallback equal-split
console.log('fallback equal:', names(shardFiles(files, 2, {
  root: '/project', hasHistory: false, strategy: 'time', fallbackStrategy: 'equal-split', affinityRules: [], isolateSlowThreshold: 0, durations,
})))

// slow isolation: slow.length < shardCount
const slowFiles = [spec('slow1.ts'), spec('slow2.ts'), spec('fast1.ts'), spec('fast2.ts')]
const d2 = new Map([['slow1.ts', 1000], ['slow2.ts', 900], ['fast1.ts', 10], ['fast2.ts', 10]])
console.log('slow iso (<count):', names(shardFiles(slowFiles, 3, {
  root: '/project', hasHistory: true, strategy: 'time', fallbackStrategy: 'hash', affinityRules: [], isolateSlowThreshold: 100, durations: d2,
})))

// slow isolation: slow.length >= shardCount
const slowFiles2 = [spec('slow1.ts'), spec('slow2.ts'), spec('slow3.ts'), spec('slow4.ts'), spec('fast1.ts')]
const d3 = new Map([['slow1.ts', 1000], ['slow2.ts', 900], ['slow3.ts', 800], ['slow4.ts', 700], ['fast1.ts', 10]])
console.log('slow iso (>=count):', names(shardFiles(slowFiles2, 3, {
  root: '/project', hasHistory: true, strategy: 'time', fallbackStrategy: 'hash', affinityRules: [], isolateSlowThreshold: 100, durations: d3,
})))

// smoothing with TTL
const history = {
  'a.ts': { duration: 100, recordedAt: 1000 },
  'b.ts': { observations: [{duration: 10, recordedAt: 1}, {duration: 20, recordedAt: 200}] },
  'legacy.ts': 5000,
}
const smoothed = getSmoothedDurations(history as any, 'latest', 100, 300)
console.log('smoothed ttl:', [...smoothed.entries()])
EOF

REASONING_CONTENT
Hmm, isolate 1/2 got a AND b, isolate 2/2 got c,d. That's not what I expected. Wait, why did shard1 get a and b?

Let me trace `isolateSlowFiles`:
- slow = [a] (304 > 200)
- remaining = [b,c,d]
- slow.length (1) < shardCount (2)
- newAssignment[0] = [a]
- remainingAssignment = shardByStrategy(remaining, 'time', ...) -> 2 shards LPT of b103,c53,d13:
  - b -> s0 (103), c -> s1 (53), d -> s1 (66)? Let's compute: b103->s0, c53->s1, d13->s1 (66). So remainingAssignment = [[b],[c,d]].
- newAssignment = [[a,b],[c,d]]

So shard1 = [a,b]. That's what we got. This is by design of my implementation: slow files are placed in first shards, and remaining files distributed
across all shards (including the shard that already has a slow file).

But wait, the spec says "Shards 1..N each get one slow file." It doesn't say the shard with a slow file can't also get remaining files. In fact with
slowCount < shardCount, "remaining" files should probably be distributed normally across all shards. So [a,b] vs [c,d] is consistent.

Hmm but is it desirable? The phrase "isolate slow files" suggests each shard gets at most one slow file (which holds here: shard1 has 1 slow a, shard2
has 0). The "remaining" files distribution isn't fully specified. My implementation is reasonable.

But let me reconsider: maybe "remaining" in the slowCount < shardCount case should be distributed to shards *that don't have slow files*? That would
make shard1=[a] and shard2=[b,c,d]. But that seems unbalanced. The spec's special case only mentions "If slow count >= shardCount, last shard gets all
extras plus remaining." It doesn't say what happens to remaining when slow count < shardCount. I'll leave as is.

Let me test the duration-history edge cases with a script:
CALL s99_c0 TOOL_ID call_00_4M6YP04ypbrCAfqgVsy73730
FUNCTION bash
ARGUMENTS
command:
cd /app && cat > test-history.tmp.ts <<'EOF'
import { writeDurationHistory, readDurationHistory, getHistoryObservations } from './packages/vitest/src/node/sequencers/duration-history'
import { mkdtempSync, writeFileSync, rmSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs'
import { join } from 'node:path'
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os'

const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'hist-'))
const file = join(dir, 'hist.json')

// legacy
writeFileSync(file, JSON.stringify({'test/a.ts': 5000}))
let h = await readDurationHistory(file)
console.log('legacy:', JSON.stringify(h))

// corrupt
writeFileSync(file, '{bad json')
console.log('corrupt null:', await readDurationHistory(file) === null)

// missing
console.log('missing null:', await readDurationHistory(join(dir, 'nope.json')) === null)

// multi-entry and write with cap
writeFileSync(file, JSON.stringify({
  'test/a.ts': { observations: [{duration: 10, recordedAt: 1000}, {duration: 20, recordedAt: 2000}] },
  'keep.ts': 42,
}))
await writeDurationHistory(file, { 'test/a.ts': 30 }, 3, 0)
h = await readDurationHistory(file)!
console.log('after write:', JSON.stringify(h))
console.log('preserved keep:', JSON.stringify(h['keep.ts']))

// cap maxRuns=2
await writeDurationHistory(file, { 'test/a.ts': 40 }, 2, 0)
h = await readDurationHistory(file)!
console.log('capped:', JSON.stringify(h['test/a.ts']))

// single mode maxRuns=1
await writeDurationHistory(file, { 'test/a.ts': 50 }, 1, 0)
h = await readDurationHistory(file)!
console.log('single:', JSON.stringify(h['test/a.ts']))

// TTL on write: old observations dropped
await writeDurationHistory(file, { 'test/a.ts': 60 }, 5, 1000)
h = await readDurationHistory(file)!
console.log('ttl write:', JSON.stringify(h['test/a.ts']))

rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true })
EOF
npx tsx test-history.tmp.ts; rm -f test-history.tmp.ts

STEP 100 SOURCE agent
REASONING_CONTENT
Duration history works:

Now let me think about remaining items and potential issues:

1. **`durationHistoryMaxRuns` cap and legacy entries with recordedAt:0**: If maxRuns=1 and legacy recordedAt=0 plus new recordedAt=now, the legacy
gets dropped (since now > 0). That's probably fine.

2. **`readDurationHistory` on malformed individual entries**: I skip invalid entries rather than return null. The spec says "Corrupt or missing:
return null." I interpreted corrupt as unparseable top-level. Individual invalid entries are skipped. That's reasonable.

3. **`recordFileDurations` in `finally`**: I call it in `runFiles` finally. But note that when `recordFileDurations` is true, it will also record
durations for files that failed or were skipped. We use `file.result?.duration`. For skipped/failed files, result may still have duration. Good. But
for files that didn't run (e.g., empty shard), they won't be in state. Good.

4. **Serialization**: added all 12 to serializeConfig. Good.

5. **`balanceShardsByTime` when shardStrategy explicitly 'time' but balanceShardsByTime false**: final strategy 'time' -> balanceShardsByTime stays
false. Good. When balanceShardsByTime true and shardStrategy explicitly 'hash', we force balanceShardsByTime false. Let me verify that behavior in a
quick test - but our validation/default logic handles it.

6. **`affinity` fallback to `time` when no rule matches any file**: In `shardAffinity`, if `matchedAny` false, returns `shardTime`. But this happens
inside `shardByStrategy` when called from `shardFiles`. In BaseSequencer, `hasHistory` true and strategy affinity -> shardAffinity; if no matches,
shardTime. Good.

But there's a subtlety: what if history is null and strategy is affinity and fallback is 'hash'? Then `shardFiles` uses strategy = 'hash', so affinity
isn't even considered. Good.

7. **`shardAffinityRules` validation**: `shardIndex` int >= 0; pattern string. Good. But the spec says "Array<{pattern: string, shardIndex: int >=
0}>". We validate.

8. **`rebalanceThreshold`**: The warning is computed on full assignment, regardless of which shard is requested. Good.

9. **`isolateSlowThreshold`**: I need to reconsider the slowCount < shardCount distribution. Let me re-read the spec once more:

"isolateSlowThreshold: split files into slow (duration > threshold) and remaining. Shards 1..N each get one slow file. If slow count >= shardCount,
last shard gets all extras plus remaining."

My implementation:
- slowCount < shardCount: slow files to shards 0..slowCount-1; remaining distributed via base strategy to ALL shards.
- slowCount >= shardCount: shards 0..count-2 one slow each; last shard gets extra slow + all remaining.

This matches the literal reading. Good.

But wait - in the slowCount >= shardCount case, "last shard gets all extras plus remaining" - I put `remaining` in last shard. Yes.

10. **`durationBasedSorting`**: sorts by duration desc, absent last. But there's an issue: the existing `sort()` in BaseSequencer also handles project
grouping/order. By short-circuiting with durationBasedSorting, we skip project/groupOrder handling. Is that acceptable? The spec says
durationBasedSorting sorts files by duration DESC; absent last. It doesn't mention preserving project grouping. Since sharding happens before sort and
projects are separate, I think it's fine for the feature. But maybe we should preserve project grouping? The spec says "sort files by duration DESC;
absent-from-history last." I'll keep as is.

Actually, wait - there's a concern. The `sort()` is also called for every group in pool (after shard), and the group may contain multiple projects.
The current sort handles project order. If durationBasedSorting short-circuits, projects might interleave. But that's what the feature asks. OK.

11. **Hash fallback equality**: When history is null and fallbackStrategy is 'hash', `shardFiles` uses `shardHash`. The `shardHash` uses
`fullPath.slice(root.length)`. The original code did the same. Existing shard tests passed. Good.

12. **CLI options**: We added new subcommands. But there's a concern: the `cli-config.ts` `CLIOptions<SequenceOptions>` now requires all new fields.
We added them. Build passed, so fine.

`slash(spec.moduleId)` is `/project/test/a.ts`, and `resolve('/project', '/project/test/a.ts')` returns `/project/test/a.ts` (resolve with absolute
second path). Good. Then `fullPath.slice(root.length)`. If root is '/project', slicing gives '/test/a.ts'. That's the key used for hashing. The
original code did `resolve(slash(config.root), slash(spec.moduleId))` and sliced. Since tests passed, behavior preserved.

- **`durationSmoothing` latest: "highest recordedAt"**: I use reduce to keep observation with max recordedAt. If tie, keeps first. Fine.

- **p95**: "sort ascending; index Math.ceil(0.95 * n) - 1". I implemented exactly. For n=0 return 0.

- **median even count: Math.floor((a+b)/2)**: I implemented. For n even, middle indices n/2-1 and n/2. For [10,30], indices 0,1 -> (10+30)/2=20. Good.

- **round-robin tie-break**: "sort DESC by duration (path ASC tie-break)". Implemented.

- **time**: "sort DESC by duration; assign to shard with lowest total; ties go to lowest-indexed shard". Implemented.

use LPT (loads from affinity-assigned files counted). If no rule matches any file, fall back to time." Implemented.

and some files match but not all? Then use affinity + LPT. Good.

says "Shards 1..N each get one slow file." If slowCount < shardCount, some shards get zero slow files - that's inherent. "remaining" presumably
distributed. My implementation distributes remaining across all shards. OK.

that's what I do.

export async function runVitest(
  config: RunVitestConfig,
  cliFilters: string[] = [],
  runnerOptions: VitestRunnerCLIOptions = {},
) {
  // Reset possible previous runs
  process.exitCode = 0
  let exitCode = process.exitCode

  if (runnerOptions.printExitCode) {
    globalThis.process = new Proxy(process_, {
      set(target, p, newValue, receiver) {
        if (p === 'exitCode') {
          // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
          console.trace('exitCode was set to', newValue)
        }
        return Reflect.set(target, p, newValue, receiver)
      },
    })
  }

  // Prevent possible process.exit() calls, e.g. from --browser
  const exit = process.exit
  process.exit = (() => { }) as never

  const stdout = new Writable({
    write(chunk, __, callback) {
      if (runnerOptions.std === 'inherit') {
        process.stdout.write(chunk.toString())
      }
      callback()
    },
  })

  if (runnerOptions?.tty) {
    (stdout as typeof process.stdout).isTTY = true
  }

  const stderr = new Writable({
    write(chunk, __, callback) {
      if (runnerOptions.std === 'inherit') {
        process.stderr.write(chunk.toString())
      }
      callback()
    },
  })

  // "node:tty".ReadStream doesn't work on Github Windows CI, let's simulate it
  const stdin = new Readable({ read: () => '' }) as NodeJS.ReadStream
  stdin.isTTY = true
  stdin.setRawMode = () => stdin
  const cli = new Cli({ stdin, stdout, stderr, preserveAnsi: runnerOptions.preserveAnsi })
  // @ts-expect-error not typed global

`duration-shard` fixture, it'll overwrite duration-history.json and break the deterministic time tests (because it'll add new observations with
current timestamps, and latest smoothing picks current run durations which are ~ms, not 400/300). So I must use a separate fixture for record test, or

rebalanceThreshold: 0.9`. Slow files: 1(400),2(300),3(200) > 150. slowCount 3 >= shardCount 2. shard0=[1], shard1=[2,3,4]. loads [400, 600], ratio

import type { TestUserConfig } from 'vitest/node'
import { basename } from 'pathe'
import { existsSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs'

import { expect, test } from 'vitest'
import * as testUtils from '../../test-utils'

function runVitest(config: TestUserConfig, root = './fixtures/duration-shard') {
  return testUtils.runVitest({ ...config, root })
}

function parsePaths(stdout: string) {
  return Array.from(new Set(stdout
    .split('\n')
    .filter(line => line && line.includes('.test.js'))
    .map(file => basename(file.trim().split(' ')[1]))
    .sort()))
}

const historyPath = 'duration-history.json'

test('time strategy balances shards by recorded duration', async () => {
  const { stdout: stdout1 } = await runVitest({
    shard: '1/2',
    sequence: { shardStrategy: 'time', durationHistoryPath: historyPath },
  })
  const { stdout: stdout2 } = await runVitest({
    shard: '2/2',
    sequence: { shardStrategy: 'time', durationHistoryPath: historyPath },
  })

  // durations: 1=400, 2=300, 3=200, 4=100
  // LPT: shard1 = [1, 4], shard2 = [2, 3]
  expect(parsePaths(stdout1)).toEqual(['1.test.js', '4.test.js'])
  expect(parsePaths(stdout2)).toEqual(['2.test.js', '3.test.js'])
})

test('round-robin strategy assigns files with a bouncing pointer', async () => {
  const { stdout: stdout1 } = await runVitest({
    shard: '1/3',
    sequence: { shardStrategy: 'round-robin', durationHistoryPath: historyPath },
  })
  const { stdout: stdout2 } = await runVitest({
    shard: '2/3',
    sequence: { shardStrategy: 'round-robin', durationHistoryPath: historyPath },
  })
  const { stdout: stdout3 } = await runVitest({
    shard: '3/3',
    sequence: { shardStrategy: 'round-robin', durationHistoryPath: historyPath },
  })

  // sorted by duration: 1, 2, 3, 4
  // pointer: 1->shard1, 2->shard2, 3->shard3, 4->shard3
  expect(parsePaths(stdout1)).toEqual(['1.test.js'])
  expect(parsePaths(stdout2)).toEqual(['2.test.js'])
  expect(parsePaths(stdout3)).toEqual(['3.test.js', '4.test.js'])
})

test('durationFallbackStrategy equal-split is used when history is missing', async () => {
  const { stdout: stdout1 } = await runVitest({
    shard: '1/2',
    sequence: {
      shardStrategy: 'time',
      durationFallbackStrategy: 'equal-split',
      durationHistoryPath: 'missing-history.json',
    },
  })
  const { stdout: stdout2 } = await runVitest({
    shard: '2/2',
    sequence: {
      shardStrategy: 'time',
      durationFallbackStrategy: 'equal-split',
      durationHistoryPath: 'missing-history.json',
    },
  })

  // sorted by path: 1, 2, 3, 4 -> shard1 = [1, 3], shard2 = [2, 4]
  expect(parsePaths(stdout1)).toEqual(['1.test.js', '3.test.js'])
  expect(parsePaths(stdout2)).toEqual(['2.test.js', '4.test.js'])
})

test('affinity rules pin files to shards and unmatched files use LPT', async () => {
  const { stdout: stdout1 } = await runVitest({
    shard: '1/2',
    sequence: {
      shardStrategy: 'affinity',
      durationHistoryPath: historyPath,
      shardAffinityRules: [
        { pattern: 'test/1.test.js', shardIndex: 1 },
        { pattern: 'test/2.test.js', shardIndex: 0 },
      ],
    },
  })
  const { stdout: stdout2 } = await runVitest({
    shard: '2/2',
    sequence: {
      shardStrategy: 'affinity',
      durationHistoryPath: historyPath,
      shardAffinityRules: [
        { pattern: 'test/1.test.js', shardIndex: 1 },
        { pattern: 'test/2.test.js', shardIndex: 0 },
      ],
    },
  })

  // 1 -> shard2, 2 -> shard1, unmatched (3, 4) via LPT with affinity loads
  expect(parsePaths(stdout1)).toEqual(['2.test.js', '3.test.js', '4.test.js'])
  expect(parsePaths(stdout2)).toEqual(['1.test.js'])
})

test('isolateSlowThreshold isolates slow files across shards', async () => {
  const { stdout: stdout1 } = await runVitest({
    shard: '1/2',
    sequence: {
      shardStrategy: 'time',
      isolateSlowThreshold: 150,
      durationHistoryPath: historyPath,
    },
  })
  const { stdout: stdout2 } = await runVitest({
    shard: '2/2',
    sequence: {
      shardStrategy: 'time',
      isolateSlowThreshold: 150,
      durationHistoryPath: historyPath,
    },
  })

  // slow files (1, 2, 3) >= shard count: shard1 gets slowest, last shard gets extras + remaining
  expect(parsePaths(stdout1)).toEqual(['1.test.js'])
  expect(parsePaths(stdout2)).toEqual(['2.test.js', '3.test.js', '4.test.js'])
})

test('rebalanceThreshold warns when shard loads are unbalanced', async () => {
  const { stdout } = await runVitest({
    shard: '1/2',
    sequence: {
      shardStrategy: 'time',
      isolateSlowThreshold: 150,
      rebalanceThreshold: 0.9,
      durationHistoryPath: historyPath,
    },
  })

  expect(stdout).toContain('ratio=0.67')
  expect(stdout).toContain('threshold=0.90')
})

test('invalid sequence config throws at startup', async () => {
  const invalid = await runVitest({
    shard: '1/2',
    sequence: { shardStrategy: 'bogus' as any, durationHistoryPath: historyPath },
  })
  expect(invalid.thrown).toBe(true)
  expect(invalid.stderr).toContain('Invalid "sequence.shardStrategy" config')

  const invalidTtl = await runVitest({
    shard: '1/2',
    sequence: { durationHistoryTTL: -1, durationHistoryPath: historyPath },
  })
  expect(invalidTtl.thrown).toBe(true)
  expect(invalidTtl.stderr).toContain('Invalid "sequence.durationHistoryTTL" config')
})

test('recordFileDurations writes a duration history file', async () => {
  const historyFile = './fixtures/duration-record/tmp-history.json'
  const historyFileAbsolute = new URL(`../fixtures/duration-record/tmp-history.json`, import.meta.url)
  rmSync(historyFileAbsolute, { force: true })

  const { thrown } = await testUtils.runVitest({
    root: './fixtures/duration-record',
    sequence: {
      recordFileDurations: true,
      durationHistoryPath: 'tmp-history.json',
      durationHistoryMaxRuns: 2,
    },
  })

  expect(thrown).toBe(false)
  expect(existsSync(historyFileAbsolute)).toBe(true)
  const history = JSON.parse(await import('node:fs/promises').then(fs => fs.readFile(historyFileAbsolute, 'utf8')))
  expect(Object.keys(history)).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['test/1.test.js', 'test/2.test.js']))
  const entry = history['test/1.test.js']
  expect(typeof entry.duration).toBe('number')
  expect(entry.recordedAt).toBeTypeOf('number')

  rmSync(historyFileAbsolute, { force: true })
})
EOF

REASONING_CONTENT
Three failures:

1. **Affinity test**: Expected shard1 = [2,3,4] but got [2,3]. Let me recompute. Rules: 1->shardIndex1 (0-based = shard 2), 2->shardIndex0 (shard 1).
Durations 1=400,2=300,3=200,4=100.
- 1 -> shard2 (load 400)
- 2 -> shard1 (load 300)
- 3 unmatched -> min load: shard1 (300) vs shard2 (400) -> shard1 (300+200=500)
- 4 unmatched -> min load: shard1(500) vs shard2(400) -> shard2 (400+100=500)
So shard1=[2,3], shard2=[1,4]. My test expectation was wrong. The implementation is correct per spec: unmatched files use LPT considering affinity
loads. Let me fix the test expectation to [2,3] and [1,4].

2. **rebalanceThreshold**: The warning goes to stderr, not stdout. Let me check `logger.console.warn` - the `console` in runVitest's Cli likely

Let me reconsider the `isolateSlowFiles` slowCount < shardCount case. In my test, I only tested slowCount >= shardCount. Let me add a test for

spec.

Actually wait, I realize there might be a subtle issue with how "remaining" is distributed when slowCount < shardCount. Let me re-read:
"isolateSlowThreshold: split files into slow (duration > threshold) and remaining. Shards 1..N each get one slow file. If slow count >= shardCount,
last shard gets all extras plus remaining."

For slowCount < shardCount: each shard gets one slow file? But there are fewer slow files than shards, so some shards get none. Then "remaining" — not
specified. My implementation distributes remaining via the base strategy to all shards. That seems reasonable.

But wait — is there a possibility that the intended behavior is: slow files are distributed one per shard, and then the remaining files are ALSO
distributed one per shard (continuing) but that's what round-robin would do. I'll leave as is.

Now let me think about another potential issue: the `isolateSlowFiles` uses `strategy` which is the *effective* strategy (after fallback). If history
is null and fallback is hash, `isolateSlowFiles` calls `shardByStrategy(remaining, 'hash', ...)`. Hash for remaining works. Good.

Now, let me reconsider `affinity` with `isolateSlowThreshold`. The `shardFiles` applies affinity first, then isolateSlowFiles. In `isolateSlowFiles`,
for slowCount < shardCount, it re-runs `shardByStrategy(remaining, 'affinity', ...)` which could fall back to time if no remaining files match
affinity rules. But the affinity assignment for remaining files would use LPT loads starting from 0, ignoring the already-assigned slow files' loads.
Hmm, that might not be ideal but it's an edge case combining two features. The spec doesn't specify interaction. Fine.

+function resolveSequenceConfig(sequence: any): void {
+  if (typeof sequence !== 'object' || sequence === null) {
+    throw new Error('Invalid "sequence" config: expected an object')
+  }
+
+  const fail = (field: string, message: string): never => {
+    throw new Error(`Invalid "sequence.${field}" config: ${message}`)
+  }
+
+  const shardStrategies = ['hash', 'time', 'round-robin', 'affinity']
+  const smoothingStrategies = ['latest', 'average', 'p95', 'median']
+  const fallbackStrategies = ['hash', 'equal-split']
+
+  if (sequence.shardStrategy !== undefined && !shardStrategies.includes(sequence.shardStrategy)) {
+    fail('shardStrategy', `expected one of "${shardStrategies.join('", "')}", received "${sequence.shardStrategy}"`)
+  }
+  if (sequence.balanceShardsByTime !== undefined && typeof sequence.balanceShardsByTime !== 'boolean') {
+    fail('balanceShardsByTime', 'expected a boolean')
+  }
+  if (sequence.recordFileDurations !== undefined && typeof sequence.recordFileDurations !== 'boolean') {
+    fail('recordFileDurations', 'expected a boolean')
+  }
+  if (sequence.durationBasedSorting !== undefined && typeof sequence.durationBasedSorting !== 'boolean') {
+    fail('durationBasedSorting', 'expected a boolean')
+  }
+  if (sequence.durationHistoryTTL !== undefined && (typeof sequence.durationHistoryTTL !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(sequence.durationHistoryTTL) || sequence.durationHistoryTTL < 0)) {
+    fail('durationHistoryTTL', 'expected a finite number greater than or equal to 0')
+  }
+  if (sequence.durationHistoryPath !== undefined) {
+    if (typeof sequence.durationHistoryPath !== 'string' || sequence.durationHistoryPath.trim() === '') {
+      fail('durationHistoryPath', 'expected a non-empty string')
+    }
+    else if (sequence.durationHistoryPath !== sequence.durationHistoryPath.trim()) {
+      fail('durationHistoryPath', 'must not have leading or trailing whitespace')
+    }
+  }
+  if (sequence.durationHistoryMaxRuns !== undefined && (!Number.isInteger(sequence.durationHistoryMaxRuns) || sequence.durationHistoryMaxRuns < 1)) {
+    fail('durationHistoryMaxRuns', 'expected an integer greater than or equal to 1')
+  }
+  if (sequence.durationSmoothing !== undefined && !smoothingStrategies.includes(sequence.durationSmoothing)) {
+    fail('durationSmoothing', `expected one of "${smoothingStrategies.join('", "')}", received "${sequence.durationSmoothing}"`)
+  }
+  if (sequence.shardAffinityRules !== undefined) {
+    if (!Array.isArray(sequence.shardAffinityRules)) {
+      fail('shardAffinityRules', 'expected an array')
+    }
+    sequence.shardAffinityRules.forEach((rule: any, index: number) => {
+      if (typeof rule !== 'object' || rule === null) {
+        fail('shardAffinityRules', `rule at index ${index} must be an object`)
+      }
+      if (typeof rule.pattern !== 'string') {
+        fail('shardAffinityRules', `rule at index ${index} must have a string "pattern"`)
+      }
+      if (!Number.isInteger(rule.shardIndex) || rule.shardIndex < 0) {
+        fail('shardAffinityRules', `rule at index ${index} must have an integer "shardIndex" greater than or equal to 0`)
+      }
+    })
+  }
+  if (sequence.rebalanceThreshold !== undefined && (typeof sequence.rebalanceThreshold !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(sequence.rebalanceThreshold) || sequence.rebalanceThreshold < 0 || sequence.rebalanceThreshold > 1)) {
+    fail('rebalanceThreshold', 'expected a number between 0 and 1 inclusive')
+  }
+  if (sequence.isolateSlowThreshold !== undefined && (typeof sequence.isolateSlowThreshold !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(sequence.isolateSlowThreshold) || sequence.isolateSlowThreshold < 0)) {
+    fail('isolateSlowThreshold', 'expected a finite number greater than or equal to 0')
+  }
+  if (sequence.durationFallbackStrategy !== undefined && !fallbackStrategies.includes(sequence.durationFallbackStrategy)) {
+    fail('durationFallbackStrategy', `expected one of "${fallbackStrategies.join('", "')}", received "${sequence.durationFallbackStrategy}"`)
+  }
+}

1. In `resolveSequenceConfig`, there's an extra blank line before `function parseInspector`. Minor style; let me remove it to match code style.

2. `readDurationHistory` uses `existsSync` synchronously then `readFile`. That's fine.

3. In `BaseSequencer.shard`, I read the history file even when strategy is `hash` and no duration features are used. That's a minor performance cost
(a file read on every shard run even for default hash). It's acceptable since the file may not exist (fast `existsSync`). But to be more efficient, we
could only read when needed. However, rebalanceThreshold, isolateSlowThreshold, and durationBasedSorting all need history. For pure default hash,
reading a missing file returns null quickly. Fine.

4. Potential issue: `shardAffinity` fallback to `time` when no rule matches — but `shardAffinity` is called from `shardByStrategy`, and
`shardByStrategy` is called from `isolateSlowFiles` for remaining files. If affinity rules don't match remaining files, it falls back to time. Good.

5. Let me reconsider the `rebalanceThreshold` check condition in `shouldWarnRebalance`: `return (minLoad / maxLoad) < threshold`. Spec says "if
minLoad / maxLoad < threshold, warn". Yes.

6. The warning message: `ratio=${ratio.toFixed(2)} threshold=${threshold.toFixed(2)}`. I have that. Good.

7. Now, there's one subtle thing about `durationHistoryTTL` and `recordFileDurations`: when TTL > 0, `writeDurationHistory` filters expired
observations at write time. The spec says "TTL (durationHistoryTTL > 0): drop observations where recordedAt < Date.now() - ttl. recordedAt === 0 never
expires." This is what I do both at read (getSmoothedDurations) and write. Good.

8. `durationHistoryMaxRuns` "cap WRITTEN observations per file (N most recent by recordedAt)". I sort by recordedAt desc and slice. But what about
`recordedAt: 0` legacy entries? They have lowest recordedAt, so with maxRuns=1 they'd be dropped when a newer entry exists. Is that desired? "N most
recent by recordedAt" - yes, legacy recordedAt 0 is oldest, so dropped. But if maxRuns=1 and only a legacy entry exists (no new run), it stays. Good.

9. Let me double check `getSmoothedDurations` for the "latest" strategy when multiple observations have the same max recordedAt. My reduce keeps the
first encountered with that max. Fine.

10. `p95` index: `Math.ceil(0.95 * n) - 1`. For n=1: ceil(0.95)-1 = 1-1=0. For n=2: ceil(1.9)-1=2-1=1 (max). For n=20: ceil(19)-1=18 (19th value,
i.e., 95th percentile-ish). Good.

Now, let me also verify the `durationBasedSorting` interaction with sharding. When `durationBasedSorting` is true but no shard, sort uses history.
Good.

Let me now consider whether the new `sequence` fields should be added to the `SequenceOptions` interface exported publicly. The interface is not
exported (`interface SequenceOptions` without export). But it's used in UserConfig and ProjectConfig which are exported. The fields are part of the
public type through `UserConfig`. Good.

Now, let me also add the new fields to any `configDefaults`? The task says default values; they're set in resolveConfig, not configDefaults.
configDefaults doesn't include sequence currently (sequence defaults are set in resolveConfig). So no change needed there.

Now, one more consideration: The task says "All 12 are serialized to worker config." I added them to serializeConfig. But there's also
`runtime/config.ts` `RuntimeConfig` which has `sequence?: { hooks?: SequenceHooks }`. That's fine.

import type { File } from '@vitest/runner'
import type { ResolvedConfig } from '../types/config'
import { existsSync } from 'node:fs'
import { mkdir, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises'
import { slash } from '@vitest/utils/helpers'
import { dirname, relative, resolve } from 'pathe'

export interface DurationObservation {
  duration: number
  recordedAt: number
}

export type DurationHistoryEntry
  = | DurationObservation
    | {
      observations: DurationObservation[]
    }
    | number

export type DurationHistory = Record<string, DurationHistoryEntry>

export function isDurationHistory(history: unknown): history is DurationHistory {
  return typeof history === 'object' && history !== null && !Array.isArray(history)
}

/**
 * Reads and normalizes the duration history file. Legacy numeric entries are
 * migrated to single-entry observations with `recordedAt: 0`.
 * Missing or corrupt files return `null`.
 */
export async function readDurationHistory(path: string): Promise<DurationHistory | null> {
  if (!existsSync(path)) {
    return null
  }

  let raw: string
  try {
    raw = await readFile(path, 'utf8')
  }
  catch {
    return null
  }

  let parsed: unknown
  try {
    parsed = JSON.parse(raw)
  }
  catch {
    return null
  }

  if (!isDurationHistory(parsed)) {
    return null
  }

  const normalized: DurationHistory = {}
  for (const [key, entry] of Object.entries(parsed)) {
    if (typeof entry === 'number') {
      normalized[key] = {
        duration: Math.round(entry),
        recordedAt: 0,
      }
    }
    else if (
      typeof entry === 'object'
      && entry !== null
      && typeof (entry as DurationObservation).duration === 'number'
      && typeof (entry as DurationObservation).recordedAt === 'number'
    ) {
      normalized[key] = {
        duration: Math.round((entry as DurationObservation).duration),
        recordedAt: (entry as DurationObservation).recordedAt,
      }
    }
    else if (
      typeof entry === 'object'
      && entry !== null
      && Array.isArray((entry as { observations?: unknown }).observations)
    ) {
      const observations = ((entry as { observations: unknown[] }).observations).filter((observation): observation is DurationObservation => (
        typeof observation === 'object'
        && observation !== null
        && typeof (observation as DurationObservation).duration === 'number'
        && typeof (observation as DurationObservation).recordedAt === 'number'
      )).map(observation => ({
        duration: Math.round(observation.duration),
        recordedAt: observation.recordedAt,
      }))
      normalized[key] = { observations }
    }
  }

  return normalized
}

/**
 * Returns all observations for a file, or an empty array when missing.
 */
export function getHistoryObservations(
  history: DurationHistory | null,
  path: string,
): DurationObservation[] {
  const entry = history?.[path]
  if (!entry) {
    return []
  }
  if (typeof entry === 'number') {
    return [{ duration: entry, recordedAt: 0 }]
  }
  if ('observations' in entry) {
    return entry.observations
  }
  return [entry]
}

/**
 * Writes duration observations to the history file. Parent directories are
 * created automatically and entries for other files are preserved.
 *
 * The number of observations written per file is capped to `maxRuns` (the N
 * most recent by `recordedAt`). When `maxRuns === 1`, a single-entry object is
 * written, otherwise a multi-entry object with `observations` is written.
 */
export async function writeDurationHistory(
  path: string,
  durations: Record<string, number>,
  maxRuns: number,
  ttl = 0,
): Promise<void> {
  const existing = await readDurationHistory(path)
  const now = Date.now()

  const merged: DurationHistory = { ...existing ?? {} }

  for (const [pathKey, duration] of Object.entries(durations)) {
    const observations = getHistoryObservations(existing, pathKey)
    observations.push({
      duration: Math.round(duration),
      recordedAt: now,
    })

    // drop expired observations, `recordedAt: 0` never expires
    const nonExpired = ttl > 0
      ? observations.filter(observation => observation.recordedAt === 0 || observation.recordedAt >= now - ttl)
      : observations

    // keep N most recent
    const capped = nonExpired
      .sort((a, b) => b.recordedAt - a.recordedAt)
      .slice(0, maxRuns)

    if (maxRuns <= 1) {
      merged[pathKey] = capped[0] ?? { duration: Math.round(duration), recordedAt: now }
    }
    else {
      merged[pathKey] = { observations: capped }
    }
  }

  await mkdir(dirname(path), { recursive: true })
  await writeFile(path, JSON.stringify(merged, null, 2), 'utf8')
}

  config: ResolvedConfig
  files: File[]
}

/**
 * Records durations of finished test files into the duration history file.
 */
export async function recordFileDurations({ config, files }: RecordFileDurationsOptions): Promise<void> {

import type { Vitest } from '../core'
import type { TestSpecification } from '../test-specification'
import type { DurationHistory } from './duration-history'
import type { TestSequencer } from './types'
import { relative, resolve } from 'pathe'
import { readDurationHistory } from './duration-history'
import { getSmoothedDurations } from './duration-smoothing'
import { compileAffinityRules } from './shard-affinity'
import {
  computeShardLoads,
  getFileRelativePath,
  shardFiles,
  shouldWarnRebalance,
} from './shard-analytics'

export class BaseSequencer implements TestSequencer {
  protected ctx: Vitest

  constructor(ctx: Vitest) {
    this.ctx = ctx
  }

  // async so it can be extended by other sequelizers
  public async shard(files: TestSpecification[]): Promise<TestSpecification[]> {
    const { config } = this.ctx
    const { index, count } = config.shard!
    const { sequence } = config

    const historyPath = resolve(config.root, sequence.durationHistoryPath)
    const history: DurationHistory | null = await readDurationHistory(historyPath)
    const durations = getSmoothedDurations(history, sequence.durationSmoothing, sequence.durationHistoryTTL)
    const affinityRules = compileAffinityRules(sequence.shardAffinityRules)

    const assignment = shardFiles(files, count, {
      root: config.root,
      hasHistory: history !== null,
      strategy: sequence.shardStrategy,
      fallbackStrategy: sequence.durationFallbackStrategy,
      affinityRules,
      isolateSlowThreshold: sequence.isolateSlowThreshold,
      durations,
    })

    if (sequence.rebalanceThreshold > 0 && history !== null) {
      const loads = computeShardLoads(assignment, config.root, durations)
      if (shouldWarnRebalance(loads, sequence.rebalanceThreshold)) {
        const minLoad = Math.min(...loads)
        const maxLoad = Math.max(...loads)
        const ratio = maxLoad > 0 ? minLoad / maxLoad : 1
        this.ctx.logger.warn(
          `Shard balance is poor: ratio=${ratio.toFixed(2)} threshold=${sequence.rebalanceThreshold.toFixed(2)}. `
          + `Consider using a duration-aware sharding strategy like "time".`,
        )
      }
    }

    return assignment[index - 1] ?? []
  }

  // async so it can be extended by other sequelizers
  public async sort(files: TestSpecification[]): Promise<TestSpecification[]> {
    const { config } = this.ctx

    if (config.sequence.durationBasedSorting) {
      const historyPath = resolve(config.root, config.sequence.durationHistoryPath)
      const history = await readDurationHistory(historyPath)
      const durations = getSmoothedDurations(history, config.sequence.durationSmoothing, config.sequence.durationHistoryTTL)

      return [...files].sort((a, b) => {
        const pathA = getFileRelativePath(config.root, a.moduleId)
        const pathB = getFileRelativePath(config.root, b.moduleId)
        const hasA = durations.has(pathA)
        const hasB = durations.has(pathB)

        // files absent from history are sorted last
        if (hasA !== hasB) {
          return hasA ? -1 : 1
        }

        const durationA = hasA ? durations.get(pathA)! : 0
        const durationB = hasB ? durations.get(pathB)! : 0
        return durationB - durationA
      })
    }

    const cache = this.ctx.cache
    return [...files].sort((a, b) => {
      // "sequence.groupOrder" is higher priority
      const groupOrderDiff = a.project.config.sequence.groupOrder - b.project.config.sequence.groupOrder
      if (groupOrderDiff !== 0) {
        return groupOrderDiff
      }

      // Projects run sequential
      if (a.project.name !== b.project.name) {
        return a.project.name < b.project.name ? -1 : 1
      }

      // Isolated run first
      if (a.project.config.isolate && !b.project.config.isolate) {
        return -1
      }
      if (!a.project.config.isolate && b.project.config.isolate) {
        return 1
      }

      const keyA = `${a.project.name}:${relative(this.ctx.config.root, a.moduleId)}`
      const keyB = `${b.project.name}:${relative(this.ctx.config.root, b.moduleId)}`

      const aState = cache.getFileTestResults(keyA)
      const bState = cache.getFileTestResults(keyB)

      if (!aState || !bState) {
        const statsA = cache.getFileStats(keyA)
        const statsB = cache.getFileStats(keyB)

        // run unknown first
        if (!statsA || !statsB) {
          return !statsA && statsB ? -1 : !statsB && statsA ? 1 : 0
        }

        // run larger files first
        return statsB.size - statsA.size
      }

      // run failed first
      if (aState.failed && !bState.failed) {
        return -1
      }
      if (!aState.failed && bState.failed) {
        return 1
      }

      // run longer first
      return bState.duration - aState.duration
    })
  }
}

export interface RecordFileDurationsOptions {
  config: ResolvedConfig
  files: File[]
}

/**
 * Records durations of finished test files into the duration history file.
 */
export async function recordFileDurations({ config, files }: RecordFileDurationsOptions): Promise<void> {
  const { sequence } = config
  if (!sequence.recordFileDurations) {
    return
  }

  const historyPath = resolve(config.root, sequence.durationHistoryPath)
  const durations: Record<string, number> = {}

  for (const file of files) {
    const duration = file.result?.duration
    if (typeof duration !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(duration)) {
      continue
    }
    const relativePath = slash(relative(config.root, file.filepath))
    durations[relativePath] = Math.round(duration)
  }

  if (Object.keys(durations).length === 0) {
    return
  }

  try {
    await writeDurationHistory(
      historyPath,
      durations,
      sequence.durationHistoryMaxRuns,
      sequence.durationHistoryTTL,
    )
  }
  catch {}
}
