import type { DurationSmoothingStrategy } from '../../runtime/config'
import type { DurationHistory, DurationObservation } from './duration-history'
import { getHistoryObservations } from './duration-history'

/**
 * Smooths a list of duration observations into a single duration value.
 */
export function smoothObservations(
  observations: DurationObservation[],
  strategy: DurationSmoothingStrategy,
): number {
  if (observations.length === 0) {
    return 0
  }

  switch (strategy) {
    case 'latest': {
      return observations.reduce((latest: DurationObservation, observation: DurationObservation) => (
        observation.recordedAt > latest.recordedAt ? observation : latest
      ), observations[0]!).duration
    }
    case 'average': {
      const sum = observations.reduce((total: number, observation: DurationObservation) => total + observation.duration, 0)
      return Math.round(sum / observations.length)
    }
    case 'p95': {
      const sorted = observations
        .map(observation => observation.duration)
        .sort((a, b) => a - b)
      const index = Math.ceil(0.95 * sorted.length) - 1
      return sorted[index]
    }
    case 'median': {
      const sorted = observations
        .map(observation => observation.duration)
        .sort((a, b) => a - b)
      const middle = Math.floor(sorted.length / 2)
      if (sorted.length % 2 === 0) {
        return Math.floor((sorted[middle - 1] + sorted[middle]) / 2)
      }
      return sorted[middle]
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Builds a map of relative file paths to smoothed durations. Expired
 * observations (based on `ttl`) are excluded. Files missing from the history
 * are not present in the returned map and should be treated as duration 0.
 */
export function getSmoothedDurations(
  history: DurationHistory | null,
  strategy: DurationSmoothingStrategy,
  ttl: number,
  now: number = Date.now(),
): Map<string, number> {
  const durations = new Map<string, number>()
  if (!history) {
    return durations
  }

  for (const path of Object.keys(history)) {
    const observations = getHistoryObservations(history, path).filter((observation: DurationObservation) => {
      if (observation.recordedAt === 0) {
        return true
      }
      return ttl <= 0 || observation.recordedAt >= now - ttl
    })

    if (observations.length > 0) {
      durations.set(path, smoothObservations(observations, strategy))
    }
  }

  return durations
}
=====AFFINITY=====
import type { ShardAffinityRule } from '../../runtime/config'
import pm from 'picomatch'

export interface CompiledAffinityRule {
  pattern: string
  shardIndex: number
  matcher: (path: string) => boolean
}

/**
 * Compiles affinity rules into matchers. Invalid patterns are ignored.
 */
export function compileAffinityRules(rules: ShardAffinityRule[]): CompiledAffinityRule[] {
  const compiled: CompiledAffinityRule[] = []
  for (const rule of rules) {
    try {
      const matcher = pm(rule.pattern)
      compiled.push({
        pattern: rule.pattern,
        shardIndex: rule.shardIndex,
        matcher,
      })
    }
    catch {
      // invalid glob pattern, skip
    }
  }
  return compiled
}

/**
 * Returns the 0-based shard index for a file, clamped to `shardCount - 1`.
 * The first matching rule wins. Returns `undefined` when no rule matches.
 */
export function getAffinityShardIndex(
  path: string,
  rules: CompiledAffinityRule[],
  shardCount: number,
): number | undefined {
  for (const rule of rules) {
    if (rule.matcher(path)) {
      return Math.min(Math.max(rule.shardIndex, 0), shardCount - 1)
    }
  }
  return undefined
}

CALL s134_c0 TOOL_ID call_00_MFEGgeBQHGpqY3f7w1Z42256
FUNCTION bash
ARGUMENTS
command:
cd /app && cat >> test/config/test/duration-shard.test.ts <<'EOF'

test('balanceShardsByTime resolves to time when shardStrategy is unset', async () => {
  const { stdout } = await runVitest({
    shard: '1/2',
    sequence: { balanceShardsByTime: true, durationHistoryPath: historyPath },
  })

  // resolved to "time": shard1 = [1, 4]
  expect(parsePaths(stdout)).toEqual(['1.test.js', '4.test.js'])
})

test('affinity falls back to time when no rule matches any file', async () => {
  const { stdout } = await runVitest({
    shard: '1/2',
    sequence: {
      shardStrategy: 'affinity',
      durationHistoryPath: historyPath,
      shardAffinityRules: [{ pattern: 'does-not-match/**', shardIndex: 0 }],
    },
  })

  // no matches -> time strategy: shard1 = [1, 4]
  expect(parsePaths(stdout)).toEqual(['1.test.js', '4.test.js'])
})

test('durationBasedSorting runs files by duration descending', async () => {
  const { stdout } = await runVitest(
    {
      sequence: {
        durationBasedSorting: true,
        durationHistoryPath: historyPath,
      },
      fileParallelism: false,
    },
    [],
    { std: 'inherit' },
  )
  const paths = stdout
    .split('\n')
    .filter(line => line && line.includes('.test.js'))
    .map(file => basename(file.trim().split(' ')[1]))

  // durations: 1=400, 2=300, 3=200, 4=100
  expect(paths).toEqual(['1.test.js', '2.test.js', '3.test.js', '4.test.js'])
})
EOF
echo done

All good. Now let me do a final review of all changes and think about any remaining gaps.

Let me reconsider the `durationHistoryPath` resolution once more. The spec says "Path: durationHistoryPath relative to project root." In
`BaseSequencer`, I use `resolve(config.root, sequence.durationHistoryPath)`. In `recordFileDurations`, same. Good.

Now, one thing I want to reconsider: The `isolateSlowFiles` function when `slow.length < shardCount` distributes remaining via `shardByStrategy` with
the *effective* strategy. But if the effective strategy is `hash` and history is present (e.g., user sets shardStrategy 'hash' +
isolateSlowThreshold), then remaining files are hash-sharded and slow files placed in first shards. This could cause some shards to have slow files
AND their hash-assigned remaining files, while others get remaining only. That's fine.

Now let me think about a subtle bug in `shardAffinity` + `isolateSlowThreshold`: when `isolateSlowFiles` is applied after affinity, it calls
`shardByStrategy(remaining, strategy='affinity', ...)`. If the affinity rules match some remaining files, they'll be pinned again; unmatched use LPT.
Good.

Now, one more thing: The `shardFiles` function receives `isolateSlowThreshold` and applies `isolateSlowFiles` after the base assignment. But in
`isolateSlowFiles`, when `slow.length < shardCount`, I rebuild the assignment from scratch using only remaining + slow. This means the base assignment
for slow files is discarded (they're redistributed), which is correct.

Now, let me think about the `rebalanceThreshold` warning ratio. The spec: "if minLoad / maxLoad < threshold, warn via ctx.logger.warn(). The message
must contain ratio=${ratio.toFixed(2)} and threshold=${threshold.toFixed(2)}." I compute ratio as min/max. Good.

import type { TestUserConfig } from 'vitest/node'
import { existsSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs'
import { basename } from 'pathe'

import { expect, test } from 'vitest'
import * as testUtils from '../../test-utils'

function runVitest(config: TestUserConfig, root = './fixtures/duration-shard') {
  return testUtils.runVitest({ ...config, root })
}

function parsePaths(stdout: string) {
  return Array.from(new Set(stdout
    .split('\n')
    .filter(line => line && line.includes('.test.js'))
    .map(file => basename(file.trim().split(' ')[1]))
    .sort()))
}

const historyPath = 'duration-history.json'

test('time strategy balances shards by recorded duration', async () => {
  const { stdout: stdout1 } = await runVitest({
    shard: '1/2',
    sequence: { shardStrategy: 'time', durationHistoryPath: historyPath },
  })
  const { stdout: stdout2 } = await runVitest({
    shard: '2/2',
    sequence: { shardStrategy: 'time', durationHistoryPath: historyPath },
  })

  // durations: 1=400, 2=300, 3=200, 4=100
  // LPT: shard1 = [1, 4], shard2 = [2, 3]
  expect(parsePaths(stdout1)).toEqual(['1.test.js', '4.test.js'])
  expect(parsePaths(stdout2)).toEqual(['2.test.js', '3.test.js'])
})

test('round-robin strategy assigns files with a bouncing pointer', async () => {
  const { stdout: stdout1 } = await runVitest({
    shard: '1/3',
    sequence: { shardStrategy: 'round-robin', durationHistoryPath: historyPath },
  })
  const { stdout: stdout2 } = await runVitest({
    shard: '2/3',
    sequence: { shardStrategy: 'round-robin', durationHistoryPath: historyPath },
  })
  const { stdout: stdout3 } = await runVitest({
    shard: '3/3',
    sequence: { shardStrategy: 'round-robin', durationHistoryPath: historyPath },
  })

  // sorted by duration: 1, 2, 3, 4
  // pointer: 1->shard1, 2->shard2, 3->shard3, 4->shard3
  expect(parsePaths(stdout1)).toEqual(['1.test.js'])
  expect(parsePaths(stdout2)).toEqual(['2.test.js'])
  expect(parsePaths(stdout3)).toEqual(['3.test.js', '4.test.js'])
})

test('durationFallbackStrategy equal-split is used when history is missing', async () => {
  const { stdout: stdout1 } = await runVitest({
    shard: '1/2',
    sequence: {
      shardStrategy: 'time',
      durationFallbackStrategy: 'equal-split',
      durationHistoryPath: 'missing-history.json',
    },
  })
  const { stdout: stdout2 } = await runVitest({
    shard: '2/2',
    sequence: {
      shardStrategy: 'time',
      durationFallbackStrategy: 'equal-split',
      durationHistoryPath: 'missing-history.json',
    },
  })

  // sorted by path: 1, 2, 3, 4 -> shard1 = [1, 3], shard2 = [2, 4]
  expect(parsePaths(stdout1)).toEqual(['1.test.js', '3.test.js'])
  expect(parsePaths(stdout2)).toEqual(['2.test.js', '4.test.js'])
})

test('affinity rules pin files to shards and unmatched files use LPT', async () => {
  const { stdout: stdout1 } = await runVitest({
    shard: '1/2',
    sequence: {
      shardStrategy: 'affinity',
      durationHistoryPath: historyPath,
      shardAffinityRules: [
        { pattern: 'test/1.test.js', shardIndex: 1 },
        { pattern: 'test/2.test.js', shardIndex: 0 },
      ],
    },
  })
  const { stdout: stdout2 } = await runVitest({
    shard: '2/2',
    sequence: {
      shardStrategy: 'affinity',
      durationHistoryPath: historyPath,
      shardAffinityRules: [
        { pattern: 'test/1.test.js', shardIndex: 1 },
        { pattern: 'test/2.test.js', shardIndex: 0 },
      ],
    },
  })

  // 1 -> shard2, 2 -> shard1, unmatched (3, 4) via LPT with affinity loads
  // 3 -> shard1 (300), 4 -> shard2 (400), so shard1 = [2, 3], shard2 = [1, 4]
  expect(parsePaths(stdout1)).toEqual(['2.test.js', '3.test.js'])
  expect(parsePaths(stdout2)).toEqual(['1.test.js', '4.test.js'])
})

test('isolateSlowThreshold isolates slow files across shards', async () => {
  const { stdout: stdout1 } = await runVitest({
    shard: '1/2',
    sequence: {
      shardStrategy: 'time',
      isolateSlowThreshold: 150,
      durationHistoryPath: historyPath,
    },
  })
  const { stdout: stdout2 } = await runVitest({
    shard: '2/2',
    sequence: {
      shardStrategy: 'time',
      isolateSlowThreshold: 150,
      durationHistoryPath: historyPath,
    },
  })

  // slow files (1, 2, 3) >= shard count: shard1 gets slowest, last shard gets extras + remaining
  expect(parsePaths(stdout1)).toEqual(['1.test.js'])
  expect(parsePaths(stdout2)).toEqual(['2.test.js', '3.test.js', '4.test.js'])
})

test('rebalanceThreshold warns when shard loads are unbalanced', async () => {
  const { stderr } = await runVitest({
    shard: '1/2',
    sequence: {
      shardStrategy: 'time',
      isolateSlowThreshold: 150,
      rebalanceThreshold: 0.9,
      durationHistoryPath: historyPath,
    },
  })

  expect(stderr).toContain('ratio=0.67')
  expect(stderr).toContain('threshold=0.90')
})

test('invalid sequence config throws at startup', async () => {
  const invalid = await runVitest({
    shard: '1/2',
    sequence: { shardStrategy: 'bogus' as any, durationHistoryPath: historyPath },
  })
  expect(invalid.thrown).toBe(true)
  expect(invalid.stderr).toContain('Invalid "sequence.shardStrategy" config')

  const invalidTtl = await runVitest({
    shard: '1/2',
    sequence: { durationHistoryTTL: -1, durationHistoryPath: historyPath },
  })
  expect(invalidTtl.thrown).toBe(true)
  expect(invalidTtl.stderr).toContain('Invalid "sequence.durationHistoryTTL" config')
})

test('recordFileDurations writes a duration history file', async () => {
  const historyFileAbsolute = new URL(`../fixtures/duration-record/tmp-history.json`, import.meta.url)
  rmSync(historyFileAbsolute, { force: true })

  const { thrown } = await testUtils.runVitest({
    root: './fixtures/duration-record',
    sequence: {
      recordFileDurations: true,
      durationHistoryPath: 'tmp-history.json',
      durationHistoryMaxRuns: 2,
    },
  })

  expect(thrown).toBe(false)
  expect(existsSync(historyFileAbsolute)).toBe(true)
  const history = JSON.parse(await import('node:fs/promises').then(fs => fs.readFile(historyFileAbsolute, 'utf8')))
  expect(Object.keys(history)).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['test/1.test.js', 'test/2.test.js']))
  const entry = history['test/1.test.js']
  const observations = Array.isArray(entry?.observations) ? entry.observations : [entry]
  expect(observations.length).toBeGreaterThan(0)
  expect(typeof observations[0].duration).toBe('number')
  expect(typeof observations[0].recordedAt).toBe('number')

  rmSync(historyFileAbsolute, { force: true })
})

test('balanceShardsByTime resolves to time when shardStrategy is unset', async () => {
  const { stdout } = await runVitest({
    shard: '1/2',
    sequence: { balanceShardsByTime: true, durationHistoryPath: historyPath },
  })

  // resolved to "time": shard1 = [1, 4]
  expect(parsePaths(stdout)).toEqual(['1.test.js', '4.test.js'])
})

test('affinity falls back to time when no rule matches any file', async () => {
  const { stdout } = await runVitest({
    shard: '1/2',
    sequence: {
      shardStrategy: 'affinity',
      durationHistoryPath: historyPath,
      shardAffinityRules: [{ pattern: 'does-not-match/**', shardIndex: 0 }],
    },
  })

  // no matches -> time strategy: shard1 = [1, 4]
  expect(parsePaths(stdout)).toEqual(['1.test.js', '4.test.js'])
})

test('durationBasedSorting runs files by duration descending', async () => {
  const { stdout } = await runVitest({
    sequence: {
      durationBasedSorting: true,
      durationHistoryPath: historyPath,
    },
    fileParallelism: false,
  })
  const paths = stdout
    .split('\n')
    .filter(line => line && line.includes('.test.js'))
    .map(file => basename(file.trim().split(' ')[1]))

  // durations: 1=400, 2=300, 3=200, 4=100
  expect(paths).toEqual(['1.test.js', '2.test.js', '3.test.js', '4.test.js'])
})
